FSR:0080 ; ===========================================================================

FSR:0080 ; Segment type: Internal processor memory & SFR
FSR:0080                 ;.segment FSR

FSR:0080                                         ; Port 0
FSR:0081                                         ; Stack Pointer

FSR:0082                                         ; Data Pointer, Low Byte

FSR:0083                                         ; Data Pointer, High Byte
FSR:0084                                         ; RESERVED
